<div class="section center clearfix">
<h2 class="title center">
            Real Apparel For Fantasy Fanatics
          </h2>
<div class="feature_divider"></div>
<p>If you play fantasy sports and wear t-shirts, you’re in the right place. Welcome to RotoWear – the first and only brand completely dedicated to bringing you the highest quality fantasy sports apparel. Whether it’s fantasy football, fantasy baseball, fantasy basketball, fantasy hockey, or DFS, we can optimize your clothing lineup. There’s a reason our gear has been seen all over social media and sports tv – it’s second to none. When it comes to designs, quality, and awesomeness, we are the consensus number one pick.</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p>Our shirts are athletic fit, which tend to be a little tighter fitting than typical, low quality t-shirts. If you are between sizes or prefer a more relaxed fit, make sure to order a size up. Chest is measured 1" below armhole. Length is measured from the highest point of the shoulder to the bottom hem of the shirt. Measurements are approximations and may vary slightly.</p>

<script type="text/javascript">
    function openNewsletterPopup() {
      setTimeout( function() {
        $('#newsletter').fancybox({wrapCSS: 'newsletter_popup', padding: [40, 40, 40, 40], scrolling: false, maxWidth: 600, minWidth: 250});
        $('#newsletter').trigger('click');
      },
      60*1000);
    }

    $(function() {
      var popup = $.cookie('popup');
      var cookie_enabled = true;

      if (cookie_enabled && popup == 'open') {
          return false;
      } else if (true || $(window).width() > 768) {
        openNewsletterPopup();
      }
      if (cookie_enabled) {
        $.cookie('popup', 'open', { expires: 7 });
      }
    });
  </script>
